<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 144G. ASSISTED LIVING</h2>
      
      <div class="sr" id="stat.144G.01"><b>144G.01</b> MS 2020 [Repealed, 2019 c 60 art 1 s 48]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.144G.02"><b>144G.02</b> MS 2020 [Repealed, 2019 c 60 art 1 s 48]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.144G.03"><b>144G.03</b> MS 2020 [Repealed, 2019 c 60 art 1 s 48]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.144G.04"><b>144G.04</b> MS 2020 [Repealed, 2019 c 60 art 1 s 48]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.144G.05"><b>144G.05</b> MS 2020 [Repealed, 2019 c 60 art 1 s 48]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.144G.06"><b>144G.06</b> MS 2020 [Repealed, 2019 c 60 art 1 s 48]</div>
      <div class="sr_by_subd" id="stat.144G.07">
        <h1>144G.07 </h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.07.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Expired, 2019 c 60 art 3 s 3]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.07.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Expired, 2019 c 60 art 3 s 3]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.07.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Expired, 2019 c 60 art 3 s 3]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.07.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Expired, 2019 c 60 art 3 s 3]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.07.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>MS 2020 [Expired, 2019 c 60 art 3 s 3]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.07.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>MS 2021 Supp [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.1.72.0">2022 c 98 art 1 s 72</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">ASSISTED LIVING LICENSURE</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.144G.08">
        <h1 class="shn">144G.08 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Applicability.</span></h2>
          <p>For the purposes of this chapter, the terms in this section have the meanings given.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Adult.</span></h2>
          <p>"Adult" means a natural person who has attained the age of 18 years.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Advanced practice registered nurse.</span></h2>
          <p>"Advanced practice registered nurse" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.171#stat.148.171.3">148.171, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Applicant.</span></h2>
          <p>"Applicant" means an individual, legal entity, or other organization that has applied for licensure under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.4a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Assisted living facility campus.</span></h2>
          <p>"Assisted living facility campus" or "campus" means:</p>
          <p>(1) a single building having two or more addresses, located on the same property with a single property identification number;</p>
          <p>(2) two or more buildings, each with a separate address, located on the same property with a single property identification number; or</p>
          <p>(3) two or more buildings at different addresses, located on properties with different property identification numbers, that share a portion of a legal property boundary.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Assisted living contract.</span></h2>
          <p>"Assisted living contract" means the legal agreement between a resident and an assisted living facility for housing and, if applicable, assisted living services.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Assisted living director.</span></h2>
          <p>"Assisted living director" means a person who administers, manages, supervises, or is in general administrative charge of an assisted living facility, whether or not the individual has an ownership interest in the facility, and whether or not the person's functions or duties are shared with one or more individuals and who is licensed by the Board of Executives for Long Term Services and Supports pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144A.20">144A.20</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Assisted living facility.</span></h2>
          <p>"Assisted living facility" means a facility that provides sleeping accommodations and assisted living services to one or more adults. Assisted living facility includes assisted living facility with dementia care, and does not include:</p>
          <p>(1) emergency shelter, transitional housing, or any other residential units serving exclusively or primarily homeless individuals, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/116L.361">116L.361</a>;</p>
          <p>(2) a nursing home licensed under chapter 144A;</p>
          <p>(3) a hospital, certified boarding care, or supervised living facility licensed under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.50">144.50</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.56">144.56</a>;</p>
          <p>(4) a lodging establishment licensed under chapter 157 and Minnesota Rules, parts <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9520.0500">9520.0500</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/rules/9520.0670">9520.0670</a>, or under chapter 245D, 245G, or 245I;</p>
          <p>(5) services and residential settings licensed under chapter 245A, including adult foster care and services and settings governed under the standards in chapter 245D;</p>
          <p>(6) a private home in which the residents are related by kinship, law, or affinity with the provider of services;</p>
          <p>(7) a duly organized condominium, cooperative, and common interest community, or owners' association of the condominium, cooperative, and common interest community where at least 80 percent of the units that comprise the condominium, cooperative, or common interest community are occupied by individuals who are the owners, members, or shareholders of the units;</p>
          <p>(8) a temporary family health care dwelling as defined in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/394.307">394.307</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/462.3593">462.3593</a>;</p>
          <p>(9) a setting offering services conducted by and for the adherents of any recognized church or religious denomination for its members exclusively through spiritual means or by prayer for healing;</p>
          <p>(10) housing financed pursuant to sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/462A.37">462A.37</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/462A.375">462A.375</a>, units financed with low-income housing tax credits pursuant to United States Code, title 26, section 42, and units financed by the Minnesota Housing Finance Agency that are intended to serve individuals with disabilities or individuals who are homeless, except for those developments that market or hold themselves out as assisted living facilities and provide assisted living services;</p>
          <p>(11) rental housing developed under United States Code, title 42, section 1437, or United States Code, title 12, section 1701q;</p>
          <p>(12) rental housing designated for occupancy by only elderly or elderly and disabled residents under United States Code, title 42, section 1437e, or rental housing for qualifying families under Code of Federal Regulations, title 24, section 983.56;</p>
          <p>(13) rental housing funded under United States Code, title 42, chapter 89, or United States Code, title 42, section 8011;</p>
          <p>(14) a covered setting as defined in section 325F.721, subdivision 1, paragraph (b); or</p>
          <p>(15) any establishment that exclusively or primarily serves as a shelter or temporary shelter for victims of domestic or any other form of violence.</p>
          <p class="see_note">[See Note.]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.7a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7a.<span class="headnote">Assisted living facility license.</span></h2>
          <p>"Assisted living facility license" or "license" means a certificate issued by the commissioner under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.10">144G.10</a> that authorizes the licensee to manage, control, and operate an assisted living facility for a specified period of time and in accordance with the terms of the license, this chapter, and the rules of the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Assisted living facility with dementia care.</span></h2>
          <p>"Assisted living facility with dementia care" means a licensed assisted living facility that is advertised, marketed, or otherwise promoted as providing specialized care for individuals with Alzheimer's disease or other dementias. An assisted living facility with a secured dementia care unit must be licensed as an assisted living facility with dementia care.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Assisted living services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Assisted living services" includes one or more of the following:</p>
          <p>(1) assisting with dressing, self-feeding, oral hygiene, hair care, grooming, toileting, and bathing;</p>
          <p>(2) providing standby assistance;</p>
          <p>(3) providing verbal or visual reminders to the resident to take regularly scheduled medication, which includes bringing the resident previously set up medication, medication in original containers, or liquid or food to accompany the medication;</p>
          <p>(4) providing verbal or visual reminders to the resident to perform regularly scheduled treatments and exercises;</p>
          <p>(5) preparing specialized diets ordered by a licensed health professional;</p>
          <p>(6) services of an advanced practice registered nurse, physician assistant, registered nurse, licensed practical nurse, physical therapist, respiratory therapist, occupational therapist, speech-language pathologist, dietitian or nutritionist, or social worker;</p>
          <p>(7) tasks delegated to unlicensed personnel by a registered nurse or assigned by a licensed health professional within the person's scope of practice;</p>
          <p>(8) medication management services;</p>
          <p>(9) hands-on assistance with transfers and mobility;</p>
          <p>(10) treatment and therapies;</p>
          <p>(11) assisting residents with eating when the residents have complicated eating problems as identified in the resident record or through an assessment such as difficulty swallowing, recurrent lung aspirations, or requiring the use of a tube or parenteral or intravenous instruments to be fed;</p>
          <p>(12) providing other complex or specialty health care services; and</p>
          <p>(13) supportive services in addition to the provision of at least one of the services listed in clauses (1) to (12).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Authority having jurisdiction.</span></h2>
          <p>"Authority having jurisdiction" means an organization, office, or individual responsible for enforcing the requirements of a code or standard, or for approving equipment, materials, an installation, or a procedure.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Authorized agent.</span></h2>
          <p>"Authorized agent" means the person who is authorized to accept service of notices and orders on behalf of the licensee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Change of ownership.</span></h2>
          <p>"Change of ownership" means a change in the licensee that is responsible for the management, control, and operation of a facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Commissioner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Commissioner" means the commissioner of health.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Controlled substance.</span></h2>
          <p>"Controlled substance" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/152.01#stat.152.01.4">152.01, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Controlling individual.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Controlling individual" means an owner and the following individuals and entities, if applicable:</p>
          <p>(1) each officer of the organization, including the chief executive officer and chief financial officer;</p>
          <p>(2) each managerial official; and</p>
          <p>(3) any entity with at least a five percent mortgage, deed of trust, or other security interest in the facility.</p>
          <p>(b) Controlling individual does not include:</p>
          <p>(1) a bank, savings bank, trust company, savings association, credit union, industrial loan and thrift company, investment banking firm, or insurance company unless the entity operates a program directly or through a subsidiary;</p>
          <p>(2) government and government-sponsored entities such as the U.S. Department of Housing and Urban Development, Ginnie Mae, Fannie Mae, Freddie Mac, and the Minnesota Housing Finance Agency which provide loans, financing, and insurance products for housing sites;</p>
          <p>(3) an individual who is a state or federal official, a state or federal employee, or a member or employee of the governing body of a political subdivision of the state or federal government that operates one or more facilities, unless the individual is also an officer, owner, or managerial official of the facility, receives remuneration from the facility, or owns any of the beneficial interests not excluded in this subdivision;</p>
          <p>(4) an individual who owns less than five percent of the outstanding common shares of a corporation:</p>
          <p>(i) whose securities are exempt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/80A.45">80A.45</a>, clause (6); or</p>
          <p>(ii) whose transactions are exempt under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/80A.46">80A.46</a>, clause (2);</p>
          <p>(5) an individual who is a member of an organization exempt from taxation under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/290.05">290.05</a>, unless the individual is also an officer, owner, or managerial official of the license or owns any of the beneficial interests not excluded in this subdivision. This clause does not exclude from the definition of controlling individual an organization that is exempt from taxation; or</p>
          <p>(6) an employee stock ownership plan trust, or a participant or board member of an employee stock ownership plan, unless the participant or board member is a controlling individual.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Dementia.</span></h2>
          <p>"Dementia" means the loss of cognitive function, including the ability to think, remember, problem solve, or reason, of sufficient severity to interfere with an individual's daily functioning. Dementia is caused by different diseases and conditions, including but not limited to Alzheimer's disease, vascular dementia, neurodegenerative conditions, Creutzfeldt-Jakob disease, and Huntington's disease.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Dementia care services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Dementia care services" means ongoing care for behavioral and psychological symptoms of dementia, including planned group and individual programming and person-centered care practices provided according to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.84">144G.84</a> to support activities of daily living for people living with dementia.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Dementia-trained staff.</span></h2>
          <p>"Dementia-trained staff" means any staff who have completed the minimum training required under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.64">144G.64</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.83">144G.83</a> and have demonstrated knowledge and the ability to support individuals with dementia.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Designated representative.</span></h2>
          <p>"Designated representative" means a person designated under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.50">144G.50</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Dietary supplement.</span></h2>
          <p>"Dietary supplement" means a product taken by mouth that contains a dietary ingredient intended to supplement the diet. Dietary ingredients may include vitamins, minerals, herbs or other botanicals, amino acids, and substances such as enzymes, organ tissue, glandulars, or metabolites.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Dietitian.</span></h2>
          <p>"Dietitian" means a person licensed as a dietitian under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.624">148.624</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.22">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Direct contact.</span></h2>
          <p>"Direct contact" means providing face-to-face care, training, supervision, counseling, consultation, or medication assistance to residents of a facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.23">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 23.<span class="headnote">Direct ownership interest.</span></h2>
          <p>"Direct ownership interest" means an individual or legal entity with the possession of at least five percent equity in capital, stock, or profits of the licensee, or who is a member of a limited liability company of the licensee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.24">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 24.<span class="headnote">Facility.</span></h2>
          <p>"Facility" means an assisted living facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.25">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 25.<span class="headnote">Hands-on assistance.</span></h2>
          <p>"Hands-on assistance" means physical help by another person without which the resident is not able to perform the activity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.26">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 26.<span class="headnote">"I'm okay" check services.</span></h2>
          <p>"'I'm okay' check services" means having, maintaining, and documenting a system to, by any means, check on the safety of a resident a minimum of once daily or more frequently according to the assisted living contract.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.27">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 27.<span class="headnote">Indirect ownership interest.</span></h2>
          <p>"Indirect ownership interest" means an individual or legal entity with a direct ownership interest in an entity that has a direct or indirect ownership interest of at least five percent in an entity that is a licensee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.28">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 28.<span class="headnote">Legal representative.</span></h2>
          <p>"Legal representative" means one of the following in the order of priority listed, to the extent the person may reasonably be identified and located:</p>
          <p>(1) a court-appointed guardian acting in accordance with the powers granted to the guardian under chapter 524;</p>
          <p>(2) a conservator acting in accordance with the powers granted to the conservator under chapter 524;</p>
          <p>(3) a health care agent acting in accordance with the powers granted to the health care agent under chapter 145C; or</p>
          <p>(4) an attorney-in-fact acting in accordance with the powers granted to the attorney-in-fact by a written power of attorney under chapter 523.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.29">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 29.<span class="headnote">Licensed health professional.</span></h2>
          <p>"Licensed health professional" means a person, other than a registered nurse or licensed practical nurse, who provides assisted living services within the scope of practice of that person's health occupation license, registration, or certification as a regulated person who is licensed by an appropriate Minnesota state board or agency.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.30">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 30.<span class="headnote">Licensed practical nurse.</span></h2>
          <p>"Licensed practical nurse" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.171#stat.148.171.8">148.171, subdivision 8</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.31">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 31.<span class="headnote">Licensed resident capacity.</span></h2>
          <p>"Licensed resident capacity" means the resident occupancy level requested by a licensee and approved by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.32">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 32.<span class="headnote">Licensee.</span></h2>
          <p>"Licensee" means a person or legal entity to whom the commissioner issues a license for an assisted living facility and who is responsible for the management, control, and operation of a facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.33">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 33.<span class="headnote">Maltreatment.</span></h2>
          <p>"Maltreatment" means conduct described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/626.5572">626.5572</a>, subdivision 15.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.34">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 34.<span class="headnote">Management agreement.</span></h2>
          <p>"Management agreement" means a written, executed agreement between a licensee and manager regarding the provision of certain services on behalf of the licensee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.35">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 35.<span class="headnote">Manager.</span></h2>
          <p>"Manager" means an individual or legal entity designated by the licensee through a management agreement to act on behalf of the licensee in the on-site management of the assisted living facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.36">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 36.<span class="headnote">Managerial official.</span></h2>
          <p>"Managerial official" means an individual who has the decision-making authority related to the operation of the facility and the responsibility for the ongoing management or direction of the policies, services, or staff of the facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.37">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 37.<span class="headnote">Medication.</span></h2>
          <p>"Medication" means a prescription or over-the-counter drug. For purposes of this chapter only, medication includes dietary supplements.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.38">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 38.<span class="headnote">Medication administration.</span></h2>
          <p>"Medication administration" means performing a set of tasks that includes the following:</p>
          <p>(1) checking the resident's medication record;</p>
          <p>(2) preparing the medication as necessary;</p>
          <p>(3) administering the medication to the resident;</p>
          <p>(4) documenting the administration or reason for not administering the medication; and</p>
          <p>(5) reporting to a registered nurse or appropriate licensed health professional any concerns about the medication, the resident, or the resident's refusal to take the medication.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.39">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 39.<span class="headnote">Medication management.</span></h2>
          <p>"Medication management" means the provision of any of the following medication-related services to a resident:</p>
          <p>(1) performing medication setup;</p>
          <p>(2) administering medications;</p>
          <p>(3) storing and securing medications;</p>
          <p>(4) documenting medication activities;</p>
          <p>(5) verifying and monitoring the effectiveness of systems to ensure safe handling and administration;</p>
          <p>(6) coordinating refills;</p>
          <p>(7) handling and implementing changes to prescriptions;</p>
          <p>(8) communicating with the pharmacy about the resident's medications; and</p>
          <p>(9) coordinating and communicating with the prescriber.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.40">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 40.<span class="headnote">Medication reconciliation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Medication reconciliation" means the process of identifying the most accurate list of all medications the resident is taking, including the name, dosage, frequency, and route, by comparing the resident record to an external list of medications obtained from the resident, hospital, prescriber, or other provider.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.41">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 41.<span class="headnote">Medication setup.</span></h2>
          <p>"Medication setup" means arranging medications by a nurse, pharmacy, or authorized prescriber for later administration by the resident or by facility staff.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.42">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 42.<span class="headnote">New construction.</span></h2>
          <p>"New construction" means a new building, renovation, modification, reconstruction, physical changes altering the use of occupancy, or addition to a building.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.43">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 43.<span class="headnote">Nurse.</span></h2>
          <p>"Nurse" means a person who is licensed under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.171">148.171</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.285">148.285</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.44">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 44.<span class="headnote">Nutritionist.</span></h2>
          <p>"Nutritionist" means a person licensed as a nutritionist under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.624">148.624</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.45">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 45.<span class="headnote">Occupational therapist.</span></h2>
          <p>"Occupational therapist" means a person who is licensed under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.6401">148.6401</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.6449">148.6449</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.46">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 46.<span class="headnote">Ombudsman.</span></h2>
          <p>"Ombudsman" means the ombudsman for long-term care.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.47">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 47.<span class="headnote">Over-the-counter drug.</span></h2>
          <p>"Over-the-counter drug" means a drug that is not required by federal law to bear the symbol "Rx only."</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.48">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 48.<span class="headnote">Owner.</span></h2>
          <p>"Owner" means an individual or legal entity that has a direct or indirect ownership interest of five percent or more in a licensee. For purposes of this chapter, "owner of a nonprofit corporation" means the president and treasurer of the board of directors or, for an entity owned by an employee stock ownership plan, means the president and treasurer of the entity. A government entity that is issued a license under this chapter shall be designated the owner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.49">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 49.<span class="headnote">Person-centered planning and service delivery.</span></h2>
          <p>"Person-centered planning and service delivery" means services as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245D.07#stat.245D.07.1a">245D.07, subdivision 1a</a>, paragraph (b).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.50">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 50.<span class="headnote">Pharmacist.</span></h2>
          <p>"Pharmacist" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/151.01">151.01, subdivision </a>3.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.51">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 51.<span class="headnote">Physical therapist.</span></h2>
          <p>"Physical therapist" means a person who is licensed under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.65">148.65</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.78">148.78</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.52">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 52.<span class="headnote">Physician.</span></h2>
          <p>"Physician" means a person who is licensed under chapter 147.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.52a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 52a.<span class="headnote">Physician assistant.</span></h2>
          <p>"Physician assistant" means a person who is licensed under chapter 147A.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.53">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 53.<span class="headnote">Prescriber.</span></h2>
          <p>"Prescriber" means a person who is authorized by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.235">148.235</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/151.01#stat.151.01.23">151.01, subdivision 23</a>; or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/151.37">151.37</a> to prescribe prescription drugs.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.54">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 54.<span class="headnote">Prescription.</span></h2>
          <p>"Prescription" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/151.01">151.01</a>, subdivision 16a.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.55">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 55.<span class="headnote">Provisional license.</span></h2>
          <p>"Provisional license" means the initial license the commissioner issues after approval of a complete written application and before the commissioner completes the provisional license survey and determines that the provisional licensee is in substantial compliance.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.56">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 56.<span class="headnote">Regularly scheduled.</span></h2>
          <p>"Regularly scheduled" means ordered or planned to be completed at predetermined times or according to a predetermined routine.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.57">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 57.<span class="headnote">Reminder.</span></h2>
          <p>"Reminder" means providing a verbal or visual reminder to a resident.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.58">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 58.<span class="headnote">Repeat violation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Repeat violation" means the issuance of two or more correction orders within a 12-month period for a violation of the same provision of a statute or rule.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.59">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 59.<span class="headnote">Resident.</span></h2>
          <p>"Resident" means an adult living in an assisted living facility who has executed an assisted living contract.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.60">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 60.<span class="headnote">Resident record.</span></h2>
          <p>"Resident record" means all records that document information about the services provided to the resident.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.61">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 61.<span class="headnote">Respiratory therapist.</span></h2>
          <p>"Respiratory therapist" means a person who is licensed under chapter 147C.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.62">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 62.<span class="headnote">Secured dementia care unit.</span></h2>
          <p>"Secured dementia care unit" means a designated area or setting designed for individuals with dementia that is locked or secured to prevent a resident from exiting, or to limit a resident's ability to exit, the secured area or setting. A secured dementia care unit is not solely an individual resident's living area.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.62a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 62a.<span class="headnote">Serious injury.</span></h2>
          <p>"Serious injury" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245.91">245.91</a>, subdivision 6.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.63">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 63.<span class="headnote">Service plan.</span></h2>
          <p>"Service plan" means the written plan between the resident and the provisional licensee or licensee about the services that will be provided to the resident.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.64">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 64.<span class="headnote">Social worker.</span></h2>
          <p>"Social worker" means a person who is licensed under chapter 148D or 148E.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.65">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 65.<span class="headnote">Speech-language pathologist. </span></h2>
          <p>"Speech-language pathologist" has the meaning given in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.512#stat.148.512.17">148.512, subdivision 17</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.66">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 66.<span class="headnote">Standby assistance.</span></h2>
          <p>"Standby assistance" means the presence of another person within arm's reach to minimize the risk of injury while performing daily activities through physical intervention or cueing to assist a resident with an assistive task by providing cues, oversight, and minimal physical assistance.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.67">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 67.<span class="headnote">Substantial compliance.</span></h2>
          <p>"Substantial compliance" means complying with the requirements in this chapter sufficiently to prevent unacceptable health or safety risks to residents.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.68">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 68.<span class="headnote">Supportive services.</span></h2>
          <p>"Supportive services" means:</p>
          <p>(1) assistance with laundry, shopping, and household chores;</p>
          <p>(2) housekeeping services;</p>
          <p>(3) provision or assistance with meals or food preparation;</p>
          <p>(4) help with arranging for, or arranging transportation to, medical, social, recreational, personal, or social services appointments;</p>
          <p>(5) provision of social or recreational services; or</p>
          <p>(6) "I'm okay" check services.</p>
          <p class="bl">Arranging for services does not include making referrals, or contacting a service provider in an emergency.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.69">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 69.<span class="headnote">Survey.</span></h2>
          <p>"Survey" means an inspection of a licensee or applicant for licensure for compliance with this chapter and applicable rules.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.70">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 70.<span class="headnote">Surveyor.</span></h2>
          <p>"Surveyor" means a staff person of the department who is authorized to conduct surveys of assisted living facilities.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.71">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 71.<span class="headnote">Treatment or therapy.</span></h2>
          <p>"Treatment" or "therapy" means the provision of care, other than medications, ordered or prescribed by a licensed health professional and provided to a resident to cure, rehabilitate, or ease symptoms.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.72">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 72.<span class="headnote">Unit of government.</span></h2>
          <p>"Unit of government" means a city, county, town, school district, other political subdivision of the state, or agency of the state or federal government, that includes any instrumentality of a unit of government.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.73">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 73.<span class="headnote">Unlicensed personnel.</span></h2>
          <p>"Unlicensed personnel" means individuals not otherwise licensed or certified by a governmental health board or agency who provide services to a resident.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.08.74">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 74.<span class="headnote">Verbal.</span></h2>
          <p>"Verbal" means oral and not in writing.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.144G.08.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/60/#laws.1.2.0">2019 c 60 art 1 s 2</a>,47; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/7/1/#laws.6.4.0">7Sp2020 c 1 art 6 s 4</a>-9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/0/30/#laws.17.6.0">2021 c 30 art 17 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/58/#laws.0.72.0">2022 c 58 s 72</a>,73; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.1.21.0">2022 c 98 art 1 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.59.41.0">2024 c 127 art 59 s 41</a>,56</p>
      </div>
      <p><b>NOTE: </b>The amendment to subdivision 7, clause (4), by Laws 2021, chapter 30, article 17, section 6, is effective upon federal approval. The commissioner of human services shall notify the revisor of statutes when federal approval is obtained. Laws 2021, chapter 30, article 17, section 114.</p>
      <div class="section" id="stat.144G.09">
        <h1 class="shn">144G.09 COMMISSIONER OVERSIGHT AND AUTHORITY OVER ASSISTED LIVING FACILITIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.09.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Regulations.</span></h2>
          <p>The commissioner shall regulate assisted living facilities pursuant to this chapter. The regulations shall include the following:</p>
          <p>(1) provisions to assure, to the extent possible, the health, safety, well-being, and appropriate treatment of residents while respecting individual autonomy and choice;</p>
          <p>(2) requirements that facilities furnish the commissioner with specified information necessary to implement this chapter;</p>
          <p>(3) standards of training of facility personnel;</p>
          <p>(4) standards for the provision of assisted living services;</p>
          <p>(5) standards for medication management;</p>
          <p>(6) standards for supervision of assisted living services;</p>
          <p>(7) standards for resident evaluation or assessment;</p>
          <p>(8) standards for treatments and therapies;</p>
          <p>(9) requirements for the involvement of a resident's health care provider, the documentation of the health care provider's orders, if required, and the resident's service plan;</p>
          <p>(10) standards for the maintenance of accurate, current resident records;</p>
          <p>(11) the establishment of levels of licenses based on services provided; and</p>
          <p>(12) provisions to enforce these regulations and the assisted living bill of rights.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.09.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Regulatory functions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall:</p>
          <p>(1) license, survey, and monitor without advance notice assisted living facilities in accordance with this chapter and rules;</p>
          <p>(2) survey every provisional licensee within one year of the provisional license issuance date subject to the provisional licensee providing assisted living services to residents;</p>
          <p>(3) survey assisted living facility licensees at least once every two years;</p>
          <p>(4) investigate complaints of assisted living facilities;</p>
          <p>(5) issue correction orders and assess civil penalties under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.30">144G.30</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.31">144G.31</a>;</p>
          <p>(6) take action as authorized in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.20">144G.20</a>; and</p>
          <p>(7) take other action reasonably required to accomplish the purposes of this chapter.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner shall review blueprints for all new facility construction and must approve the plans before construction may be commenced.</p>
          <p>(c) The commissioner shall provide on-site review of the construction to ensure that all physical environment standards are met before the facility license is complete.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.09.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Rulemaking authorized.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The commissioner shall adopt rules for all assisted living facilities that promote person-centered planning and service delivery and optimal quality of life, and that ensure resident rights are protected, resident choice is allowed, and public health and safety is ensured.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) On July 1, 2019, the commissioner shall begin rulemaking.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The commissioner shall adopt rules that include but are not limited to the following:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) staffing appropriate for each licensure category to best protect the health and safety of residents no matter their vulnerability;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) training prerequisites and ongoing training, including dementia care training and standards for demonstrating competency;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) procedures for discharge planning and ensuring resident appeal rights;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) initial assessments, continuing assessments, and a uniform assessment tool;</p>
          <p>(5) emergency disaster and preparedness plans;</p>
          <p>(6) uniform checklist disclosure of services;</p>
          <p>(7) a definition of serious injury that results from maltreatment;</p>
          <p>(8) conditions and fine amounts for planned closures;</p>
          <p>(9) procedures and timelines for the commissioner regarding termination appeals between facilities and the Office of Administrative Hearings;</p>
          <p>(10) establishing base fees and per-resident fees for each category of licensure;</p>
          <p>(11) considering the establishment of a maximum amount for any one fee;</p>
          <p>(12) procedures for relinquishing an assisted living facility with dementia care license and fine amounts for noncompliance; and</p>
          <p>(13) procedures to efficiently transfer existing housing with services registrants and home care licensees to the new assisted living facility licensure structure.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The commissioner shall publish the proposed rules by December 31, 2019, and shall publish final rules by December 31, 2020.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.144G.09.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/60/#laws.1.34.0">2019 c 60 art 1 s 34</a>,41,47</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.144G.10">
        <h1 class="shn">144G.10 ASSISTED LIVING FACILITY LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.10.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">License required.</span></h2>
          <p>(a)(1) Beginning August 1, 2021, no assisted living facility may operate in Minnesota unless it is licensed under this chapter.</p>
          <p>(2) No facility or building on a campus may provide assisted living services until obtaining the required license under paragraphs (c) to (e).</p>
          <p>(b) The licensee is legally responsible for the management, control, and operation of the facility, regardless of the existence of a management agreement or subcontract. Nothing in this chapter shall in any way affect the rights and remedies available under other law.</p>
          <p>(c) Upon approving an application for an assisted living facility license, the commissioner shall issue a single license for each building that is operated by the licensee as an assisted living facility and is located at a separate address, except as provided under paragraph (d) or (e).</p>
          <p>(d) Upon approving an application for an assisted living facility license, the commissioner may issue a single license for two or more buildings on a campus that are operated by the same licensee as an assisted living facility. An assisted living facility license for a campus must identify the address and licensed resident capacity of each building located on the campus in which assisted living services are provided.</p>
          <p>(e) Upon approving an application for an assisted living facility license, the commissioner may:</p>
          <p>(1) issue a single license for two or more buildings on a campus that are operated by the same licensee as an assisted living facility with dementia care, provided the assisted living facility for dementia care license for a campus identifies the buildings operating as assisted living facilities with dementia care; or</p>
          <p>(2) issue a separate assisted living facility with dementia care license for a building that is on a campus and that is operating as an assisted living facility with dementia care.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.10.1a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Assisted living director license required.</span></h2>
          <p>Each assisted living facility must employ an assisted living director licensed or permitted by the Board of Executives for Long Term Services and Supports.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.10.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Licensure categories.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The categories in this subdivision are established for assisted living facility licensure.</p>
          <p>(1) The assisted living facility category is for assisted living facilities that only provide assisted living services.</p>
          <p>(2) The assisted living facility with dementia care category is for assisted living facilities that provide assisted living services and dementia care services. An assisted living facility with dementia care may also provide dementia care services in a secured dementia care unit.</p>
          <p>(b) An assisted living facility that has a secured dementia care unit must be licensed as an assisted living facility with dementia care.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.10.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Licensure under other law.</span></h2>
          <p>An assisted living facility licensed under this chapter is not required to also be licensed as a boarding establishment, food and beverage service establishment, hotel, motel, lodging establishment, resort, or restaurant under chapter 157.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.10.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Violations; penalty.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Operating an assisted living facility without a license is a misdemeanor, and the commissioner may also impose a fine.</p>
          <p>(b) A controlling individual of the facility in violation of this section is guilty of a misdemeanor. This paragraph shall not apply to any controlling individual who had no legal authority to affect or change decisions related to the operation of the facility.</p>
          <p>(c) The sanctions in this section do not restrict other available sanctions in law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.10.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Protected title; restriction on use.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Effective January 1, 2026, no person or entity may use the phrase "assisted living," whether alone or in combination with other words and whether orally or in writing, to: advertise; market; or otherwise describe, offer, or promote itself, or any housing, service, service package, or program that it provides within this state, unless the person or entity is a licensed assisted living facility that meets the requirements of this chapter. A person or entity entitled to use the phrase "assisted living" shall use the phrase only in the context of its participation that meets the requirements of this chapter.</p>
          <p>(b) Effective January 1, 2026, the licensee's name for a new assisted living facility may not include the terms "home care" or "nursing home."</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.144G.10.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/60/#laws.1.3.0">2019 c 60 art 1 s 3</a>,47; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/7/1/#laws.6.10.0">7Sp2020 c 1 art 6 s 10</a>,11; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.59.42.0">2024 c 127 art 59 s 42</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.144G.11">
        <h1 class="shn">144G.11 APPLICABILITY OF OTHER LAWS.</h1>
        <p>Assisted living facilities:</p>
        <p>(1) are subject to and must comply with chapter 504B;</p>
        <p>(2) must comply with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/325F.72">325F.72</a>; and</p>
        <p>(3) are not required to obtain a lodging license under chapter 157 and related rules.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.144G.11.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/60/#laws.1.44.0">2019 c 60 art 1 s 44</a>,47</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.144G.12">
        <h1 class="shn">144G.12 APPLICATION FOR LICENSURE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.12.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">License applications.</span></h2>
          <p>Each application for an assisted living facility license, including provisional and renewal applications, must include information sufficient to show that the applicant meets the requirements of licensure, including:</p>
          <p>(1) the business name and legal entity name of the licensee, and the street address and mailing address of the facility;</p>
          <p>(2) the names, email addresses, telephone numbers, and mailing addresses of all owners, controlling individuals, managerial officials, and the assisted living director;</p>
          <p>(3) the name and email address of the managing agent and manager, if applicable;</p>
          <p>(4) the licensed resident capacity and the license category;</p>
          <p>(5) the license fee in the amount specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.122">144.122</a>;</p>
          <p>(6) documentation of compliance with the background study requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.13">144G.13</a> for the owner, controlling individuals, and managerial officials. Each application for a new license must include documentation for the applicant and for each individual with five percent or more direct or indirect ownership in the applicant;</p>
          <p>(7) evidence of workers' compensation coverage as required by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/176.181">176.181</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/176.182">176.182</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) documentation that the facility has liability coverage;</p>
          <p class="in">(9) a copy of the executed lease agreement between the landlord and the licensee, if applicable;</p>
          <p class="in">(10) a copy of the management agreement, if applicable;</p>
          <p class="in">(11) a copy of the operations transfer agreement or similar agreement, if applicable;</p>
          <p class="in">(12) an organizational chart that identifies all organizations and individuals with an ownership interest in the licensee of five percent or greater and that specifies their relationship with the licensee and with each other;</p>
          <p>(13) whether the applicant, owner, controlling individual, managerial official, or assisted living director of the facility has ever been convicted of:</p>
          <p>(i) a crime or found civilly liable for a federal or state felony level offense that was detrimental to the best interests of the facility and its resident within the last ten years preceding submission of the license application. Offenses include: felony crimes against persons and other similar crimes for which the individual was convicted, including guilty pleas and adjudicated pretrial diversions; financial crimes such as extortion, embezzlement, income tax evasion, insurance fraud, and other similar crimes for which the individual was convicted, including guilty pleas and adjudicated pretrial diversions; any felonies involving malpractice that resulted in a conviction of criminal neglect or misconduct; and any felonies that would result in a mandatory exclusion under section 1128(a) of the Social Security Act;</p>
          <p>(ii) any misdemeanor conviction, under federal or state law, related to: the delivery of an item or service under Medicaid or a state health care program, or the abuse or neglect of a patient in connection with the delivery of a health care item or service;</p>
          <p>(iii) any misdemeanor conviction, under federal or state law, related to theft, fraud, embezzlement, breach of fiduciary duty, or other financial misconduct in connection with the delivery of a health care item or service;</p>
          <p>(iv) any felony or misdemeanor conviction, under federal or state law, relating to the interference with or obstruction of any investigation into any criminal offense described in Code of Federal Regulations, title 42, section 1001.101 or 1001.201;</p>
          <p>(v) any felony or misdemeanor conviction, under federal or state law, relating to the unlawful manufacture, distribution, prescription, or dispensing of a controlled substance;</p>
          <p>(vi) any felony or gross misdemeanor that relates to the operation of a nursing home or assisted living facility or directly affects resident safety or care during that period;</p>
          <p>(vii) any revocation or suspension of a license to provide health care by any state licensing authority. This includes the surrender of such a license while a formal disciplinary proceeding was pending before a state licensing authority;</p>
          <p>(viii) any revocation or suspension of accreditation; or</p>
          <p>(ix) any suspension or exclusion from participation in, or any sanction imposed by, a federal or state health care program, or any debarment from participation in any federal executive branch procurement or nonprocurement program;</p>
          <p class="in">(14) whether, in the preceding three years, the applicant or any owner, controlling individual, managerial official, or assisted living director of the facility has a record of defaulting in the payment of money collected for others, including the discharge of debts through bankruptcy proceedings;</p>
          <p class="in">(15) the signature of the owner of the licensee, or an authorized agent of the licensee;</p>
          <p>(16) identification of all states where the applicant or individual having a five percent or more ownership, currently or previously has been licensed as an owner or operator of a long-term care, community-based, or health care facility or agency where its license or federal certification has been denied, suspended, restricted, conditioned, refused, not renewed, or revoked under a private or state-controlled receivership, or where these same actions are pending under the laws of any state or federal authority;</p>
          <p>(17) statistical information required by the commissioner; and</p>
          <p>(18) any other information required by the commissioner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.12.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Authorized agents.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An application for an assisted living facility license or for renewal of a facility license must specify one or more owners, controlling individuals, or employees as authorized agents who can accept service on behalf of the licensee in proceedings under this chapter.</p>
          <p>(b) Notwithstanding any law to the contrary, personal service on the authorized agent named in the application is deemed to be service on all of the controlling individuals or managerial officials of the facility, and it is not a defense to any action arising under this chapter that personal service was not made on each controlling individual or managerial official of the facility. The designation of one or more controlling individuals or managerial officials under this subdivision shall not affect the legal responsibility of any other controlling individual or managerial official under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.12.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Fees.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An initial applicant, renewal applicant, or applicant filing a change of ownership for assisted living facility licensure must submit the application fee required in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.122">144.122</a> to the commissioner along with a completed application.</p>
          <p>(b) Fees collected under this section shall be deposited in the state treasury and credited to the state government special revenue fund. All fees are nonrefundable.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.12.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Fines and penalties.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The penalty for late submission of the renewal application less than 30 days before the expiration date of the license or after expiration of the license is $200. The penalty for operating a facility after expiration of the license and before a renewal license is issued is $250 each day after expiration of the license until the renewal license issuance date. The facility is still subject to the misdemeanor penalties for operating after license expiration.</p>
          <p>(b) Fines and penalties collected under this subdivision shall be deposited in a dedicated special revenue account. On an annual basis, the balance in the special revenue account shall be appropriated to the commissioner to implement the recommendations of the advisory council established in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144A.4799">144A.4799</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.144G.12.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/60/#laws.1.5.0">2019 c 60 art 1 s 5</a>,47</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.144G.13">
        <h1 class="shn">144G.13 BACKGROUND STUDIES OF LICENSE APPLICANTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.13.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Background studies required.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Before the commissioner issues a provisional license, issues a license as a result of an approved change of ownership, or renews a license, a managerial official or a natural person who is an owner with direct ownership interest is required to undergo a background study under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.057">144.057</a>. No person may be involved in the management, operation, or control of an assisted living facility if the person has been disqualified under chapter 245C. For the purposes of this section, managerial officials subject to the background study requirement are individuals who provide direct contact. Nothing in this section shall be construed to prohibit the facility from requiring self-disclosure of criminal conviction information.</p>
          <p>(b) The commissioner shall not issue a license if any controlling individual, including a managerial official, has been unsuccessful in having a background study disqualification set aside under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.057">144.057</a> and chapter 245C.</p>
          <p>(c) Termination of staff in good faith reliance on information or records obtained under this section regarding a confirmed conviction does not subject the assisted living facility to civil liability or liability for unemployment benefits.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.13.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Reconsideration.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If the individual requests reconsideration of a disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144.057">144.057</a> or chapter 245C and the commissioner sets aside or rescinds the disqualification, the individual is eligible to be involved in the management, operation, or control of the facility.</p>
          <p>(b) If an individual has a disqualification under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/245C.15">245C.15</a>, subdivision 1, and the disqualification is affirmed, the individual's disqualification is barred from a set aside, and the individual must not be involved in the management, operation, or control of the facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.13.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Data classification.</span></h2>
          <p>Data collected under this section shall be classified as private data on individuals under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/13.02#stat.13.02.12">13.02, subdivision 12</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.144G.13.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/60/#laws.1.7.0">2019 c 60 art 1 s 7</a>,47; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.59.56.0">2024 c 127 art 59 s 56</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.144G.15">
        <h1 class="shn">144G.15 CONSIDERATION OF APPLICATIONS.</h1>
        <p>(a) Before issuing a provisional license or license or renewing a license, the commissioner shall consider an applicant's compliance history in providing care in this state or any other state in a facility that provides care to children, the elderly, ill individuals, or individuals with disabilities.</p>
        <p>(b) The applicant's compliance history shall include repeat violation, rule violations, and any license or certification involuntarily suspended or terminated during an enforcement process.</p>
        <p>(c) The commissioner may deny, revoke, suspend, restrict, or refuse to renew the license or impose conditions if:</p>
        <p>(1) the applicant fails to provide complete and accurate information on the application and the commissioner concludes that the missing or corrected information is needed to determine if a license shall be granted;</p>
        <p>(2) the applicant, knowingly or with reason to know, made a false statement of a material fact in an application for the license or any data attached to the application or in any matter under investigation by the department;</p>
        <p>(3) the applicant refused to allow agents of the commissioner to inspect its books, records, and files related to the license application, or any portion of the premises;</p>
        <p>(4) the applicant willfully prevented, interfered with, or attempted to impede in any way: (i) the work of any authorized representative of the commissioner, the ombudsman for long-term care, or the ombudsman for mental health and developmental disabilities; or (ii) the duties of the commissioner, local law enforcement, city or county attorneys, adult protection, county case managers, or other local government personnel;</p>
        <p>(5) the applicant, owner, controlling individual, managerial official, or assisted living director for the facility has a history of noncompliance with federal or state regulations that were detrimental to the health, welfare, or safety of a resident or a client; or</p>
        <p>(6) the applicant violates any requirement in this chapter.</p>
        <p>(d) If a license is denied, the applicant has the reconsideration rights available under section 144G.16, subdivision 4.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.144G.15.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/60/#laws.1.10.0">2019 c 60 art 1 s 10</a>,47; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.1.22.0">2022 c 98 art 1 s 22</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.144G.16">
        <h1 class="shn">144G.16 PROVISIONAL LICENSE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.16.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Provisional license.</span></h2>
          <p>Beginning August 1, 2021, for new assisted living facility license applicants, the commissioner shall issue a provisional license from one of the licensure categories specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.10">144G.10</a>, subdivision 2. A provisional license is effective for up to one year from the initial effective date of the license, except that a provisional license may be extended according to subdivisions 2, paragraph (d), and 3.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.16.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Initial survey.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) During the provisional license period, the commissioner shall survey the provisional licensee after the commissioner is notified or has evidence that the provisional licensee is providing assisted living services to at least one resident.</p>
          <p>(b) Within two days of beginning to provide assisted living services, the provisional licensee must provide notice to the commissioner that it is providing assisted living services by sending an email to the email address provided by the commissioner.</p>
          <p>(c) If the provisional licensee does not provide services during the provisional license period, the provisional license shall expire at the end of the period and the applicant must reapply.</p>
          <p>(d) If the provisional licensee notifies the commissioner that the licensee is providing assisted living services within 45 calendar days prior to expiration of the provisional license, the commissioner may extend the provisional license for up to 60 calendar days in order to allow the commissioner to complete the on-site survey required under this section and follow-up survey visits.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.16.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Licensure; termination or extension of provisional licenses.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If the provisional licensee is in substantial compliance with the survey, the commissioner shall issue a facility license.</p>
          <p>(b) If the provisional licensee is not in substantial compliance with the initial survey, the commissioner shall either: (1) not issue the facility license and terminate the provisional license; or (2) extend the provisional license for a period not to exceed 90 calendar days and apply conditions necessary to bring the facility into substantial compliance. If the provisional licensee is not in substantial compliance with the survey within the time period of the extension or if the provisional licensee does not satisfy the license conditions, the commissioner may deny the license.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.16.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Reconsideration.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If a provisional licensee whose assisted living facility license has been denied or extended with conditions disagrees with the conclusions of the commissioner, then the provisional licensee may request a reconsideration by the commissioner. The reconsideration request process must be conducted internally by the commissioner and chapter 14 does not apply.</p>
          <p>(b) The provisional licensee requesting the reconsideration must make the request in writing and must list and describe the reasons why the provisional licensee disagrees with the decision to deny the facility license or the decision to extend the provisional license with conditions.</p>
          <p>(c) The reconsideration request and supporting documentation must be received by the commissioner within 15 calendar days after the date the provisional licensee receives the denial or provisional license with conditions.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.16.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Continued operation.</span></h2>
          <p>A provisional licensee whose license is denied is permitted to continue operating during the period of time when:</p>
          <p>(1) a reconsideration is in process;</p>
          <p>(2) an extension of the provisional license and terms associated with it is in active negotiation between the commissioner and the licensee, and the commissioner confirms the negotiation is active; or</p>
          <p>(3) a transfer of residents to a new facility is underway and not all of the residents have relocated.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.16.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Requirements for notice and transfer.</span></h2>
          <p>A provisional licensee whose license is denied must comply with the requirements for notification and the coordinated move of residents in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.52">144G.52</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.55">144G.55</a>. If the license denial is upheld by the reconsideration process, the licensee must submit a draft closure plan as required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.57">144G.57</a> within ten calendar days of receipt of the reconsideration decision, must work with the commissioner on any revisions needed to the draft plan, and must have a final closure plan submitted and approved within 30 calendar days of receipt of the reconsideration decision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.16.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Fines and penalties.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The fine for failure to comply with the notification requirements in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.52#stat.144G.52.7">144G.52, subdivision 7</a>, is $1,000.</p>
          <p>(b) Fines and penalties collected under this section shall be deposited in a dedicated special revenue account. On an annual basis, the balance in the special revenue account shall be appropriated to the commissioner to implement the recommendations of the advisory council established in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144A.4799">144A.4799</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.144G.16.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/60/#laws.1.4.0">2019 c 60 art 1 s 4</a>,47; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.4.47.0">2023 c 70 art 4 s 47</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.59.43.0">2024 c 127 art 59 s 43</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.144G.17">
        <h1 class="shn">144G.17 LICENSE RENEWAL.</h1>
        <p>A license that is not a provisional license may be renewed for a period of up to one year if the licensee:</p>
        <p>(1) submits an application for renewal in the format provided by the commissioner at least 60 calendar days before expiration of the license;</p>
        <p>(2) submits the renewal fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.12#stat.144G.12.3">144G.12, subdivision 3</a>;</p>
        <p>(3) submits the late fee under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.12#stat.144G.12.4">144G.12, subdivision 4</a>, if the renewal application is received less than 30 days before the expiration date of the license or after the expiration of the license;</p>
        <p>(4) provides information sufficient to show that the applicant meets the requirements of licensure, including items required under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.12#stat.144G.12.1">144G.12, subdivision 1</a>;</p>
        <p>(5) provides information sufficient to show the licensee provided assisted living services to at least one resident during the immediately preceding license year and at the assisted living facility listed on the license; and</p>
        <p>(6) provides any other information deemed necessary by the commissioner.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.144G.17.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/60/#laws.1.8.0">2019 c 60 art 1 s 8</a>,47; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.1.23.0">2022 c 98 art 1 s 23</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.144G.18">
        <h1 class="shn">144G.18 NOTIFICATION OF CHANGES IN INFORMATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.18.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Notification.</span></h2>
          <p>A provisional licensee or licensee shall notify the commissioner in writing prior to a change in the manager or authorized agent and within 60 calendar days after any change in the information required in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144G.12#stat.144G.12.1">144G.12, subdivision 1</a>, clause (1), (3), (4), (17), or (18).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.144G.18.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Fines and penalties.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The fine for failure to comply with the notification requirements of this section is $1,000.</p>
          <p>(b) Fines and penalties collected under this subdivision shall be deposited in a dedicated special revenue account. On an annual basis, the balance in the special revenue account shall be appropriated to the commissioner to implement the recommendations of the advisory council established in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/144A.4799">144A.4799</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.144G.18.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/0/60/#laws.1.9.0">2019 c 60 art 1 s 9</a>,47; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/7/1/#laws.6.25.0">7Sp2020 c 1 art 6 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/70/#laws.4.48.0">2023 c 70 art 4 s 48</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.144G.19">
        <h1 class="shn">144G.19 TRANSFER OF LICENSE PROHIBITED.</h1>
